/***
REF
---

SHORTHAND

$str@[addr] string starting at addr
<[addr]> Sets the address and doesn't reset it
<EXIT> Path has an EXIT (or NEW_ECL, which also exits)
<RETURN> Path has A RETURN
<LOOP> Path has a looping reference

OPS

COMBAT:
	monstersLoaded == false && combat_type == normal
		? gbvmEnterShop
			? CityShop()
			: gbvmEnterTemple
				? TempleShop()
				: AfterCombatExpAndTreasure()
		: DoCombat()

LOAD_CHARACTER (charId):
	sets curChar to the character at index charId
	if bit 7, dumpOnStatusClear, dumpOnNameClear all set, removes character from party
	EXIT and PROGRAM restore previous character to curChar

LOAD_FILES (fileId, unused, loadTiles):
	filesLoaded = true
	mapLoaded = true
	fileId != 255 && fileId != 127 && gd.view3dFlag != 0
		? LoadGeo(fileId); moveLocked = 0
	loadTiles != 255 && gd.view3dFlag == 0
		? LoadTiles(0, 40)

LOAD_PIECES (wallSetId1, wallSetId2, wallSetId3):
	filesLoaded = true
	piecesLoaded = true
	wallSetId1 == 127
		? LoadWallSet(0, 1)
		: gd.mapGraphics1 != 0 && gd.mapGraphics2 != 0
			? LoadWallSet(wallSetId1, 1); LoadWallSet(wallSetId3, 3)
			: i : 1 to 3:
				param[i] != 255
					? LoadWallSet(param[i], i)
					: gd.wallSetCache[i].wallDefId = -1; gd.wallSetCache[i].symbolSlot = -1

***/
